# Whole-repository read, second pass (uncapped) — 2026-10-02

**What this is.** The first read ([`WHOLE_REPO_READ_2026-10-01.md`](WHOLE_REPO_READ_2026-10-01.md)) capped every reader at 12 findings and said so: 605 were seen and not returned. The Owner asked for the cap to be lifted. This file records what the uncapped readers returned for the part that has been re-read so far. It is a list of findings, not of fixes: **every item below is OPEN** unless its line says otherwise.

## How it was run

- **35 slices** of the first read's 77 were re-read without a cap — the Python, shell, workflow, gate and document slices — plus **two sweeps** (`doc-vs-doc`, `dup-python`). They returned 1311 findings.
- Every finding went to a second agent told to refute it by opening the cited line: **1248 confirmed, 63 refuted**. Of the confirmed, 22 were already in the first record and are left out here. **1226 remain** and are listed below.
- **Not re-read in this pass:** the Rust and frontend slices and the sweeps `dup-rust`, `doc-vs-code`, `dead-and-placeholder`, `test-quality`. Nothing in this file speaks for them; they were read later the same day and are recorded in [`WHOLE_REPO_READ_2026-10-02_COCKPIT.md`](WHOLE_REPO_READ_2026-10-02_COCKPIT.md).
- "Confirmed" means a second agent agreed with a first after reading the line. It is ◑, not ✅. The Builder personally reproduced only the items marked **reproduced** in §1.
- The full text of each finding — evidence, the verifier's note, the other location of a duplicate — is in [`archive/whole_repo_read_2_2026-10-02.json`](archive/whole_repo_read_2_2026-10-02.json), keyed by the `R2-` id used here.

## The numbers

- By severity: 3 high · 105 medium · 1118 low.
- By kind: 295 duplicate · 284 stale-claim · 182 design-contradiction · 170 bug · 149 test-defect · 76 dead-code · 54 security · 16 other.

## 1. High

### R2-0001 · `engine/runtime/bro_security.py:185` — split_shell does not model shell comments: a mutation (or a git push) on the next line rides a read-only `echo`

- Evidence: Ran classify_tool_action('Bash', "echo hi # ' \n rm -rf src #'") -> action echo:hi, caps ('READ_LOCAL',), mutating=False, push=False, targets=(). The quote opened inside the comment swallows the newline. bash ran the second line of the same shape (printed INJECTED2).
- Second agent: Reproduced on the current tree: classify_tool_action('Bash', {'command': "echo hi # ' \n rm -rf src #'"}) returns action echo:hi, caps READ_LOCAL, mutating=False, targets=(); bash ran the second line of the same shape with a harmless echo (printed INJECTED2). split_shell (bro_security.py:180-240) has no comment handling. Classifier plus bash only: I did not drive the full pre-tool hook. The recorded `&` entry is a different spelling.
- Suggested fix: Refuse an unquoted `#` at a word start (or any newline inside a quote) in split_shell, and add both spellings as deny tests.
- **Fixed in `#333` (T-159):** `split_shell` is gone. `parse_simple_command` accepts one simple command and refuses `#` and a newline anywhere outside its language; the Architect audited it, GREEN on the second round.

### R2-0002 · `engine/runtime/bro_security.py:195` — split_shell does not model bash ANSI-C quoting ($'...'): three commands are classified as one read-only echo

- Evidence: Ran classify_tool_action('Bash', "echo $'\\'' ; rm -rf src ; echo \\'") -> one CommandInfo executable='echo', mutating=False, targets=(), caps READ_LOCAL. bash -c of the same shape with a harmless middle command printed INJECTED-RAN: bash ends the string at the 3rd quote, the parser does not.
- Second agent: Reproduced: classify_tool_action('Bash', {'command': "echo $'\\'' ; rm -rf src ; echo \\'"}) returns a single echo CommandInfo, READ_LOCAL, mutating=False; bash -c of the same shape with a harmless middle command printed INJECTED-RAN. split_shell refuses `$(` but not `$'`. Classifier plus bash only: the full hook was not driven.
- Suggested fix: Refuse `$'` and `$"` outside single quotes in split_shell, as `$(` already is.
- **Fixed in `#333` (T-159):** `$` is refused outside single quotes and inside double quotes, so neither `$'...'` nor `$"..."` reaches the classifier.

### R2-0003 · `docs/OPERATOR_GUIDE.md:342` — Operator guide says agent-mode Bash is bounded by a deny list; the deny list is empty by Owner decision

- Evidence: Guide :342 'Bash is bounded by a deny list — no delete, no `git push`, no dependency install, no nested shell'; :373 'a sandbox, a bounded shell'. ai.rs:1203 `const BRO_BASH_DENY: &[&str] = &[];`, ai.rs:1847 'The shell is not bounded'. SECURITY.md says agent mode runs in the project dir, not the sandbox.
- Second agent: docs/OPERATOR_GUIDE.md:342-343 says 'Bash is bounded by a deny list — no delete, no git push, no dependency install, no nested shell' and :373 'a bounded shell'; apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:1203 is `const BRO_BASH_DENY: &[&str] = &[];` and ai.rs:1847 says 'The shell is not bounded.' The operator-facing doc describes a control that does not exist.
- Suggested fix: Rewrite §5/§6/§11 to say the shell deny-list is empty since 2026-08-20 and only trust-surface writes and CLI built-in agents are refused, as apps/desktop/SECURITY.md already does.

- **R2-0036** `docs/design/WAVE_3B_ISOLATED_SIGNER_DESIGN.md:557` — Manifest signed bytes are specified as JCS with base64url keys and a protocol tag; the code signs declaration-order JSON with hex keys and no tag *Evidence:* :557 "`root_sig` is a detached Ed25519 signature over `JCS(payload)`", :536 `manifest_protocol:"brops.key-manifest.v1"`, :541 `public_key:<b64url 32B>`. key_manifest.rs:54 "NOT sorted-key JSON ... The declaration order IS the signed form"; `public_key_hex`; `brops.key-manifest.v1` has 0 occurrences… *Fix:* Extend the existing NOT IMPLEMENTED note to §4.3 so the design stops stating a canonical form the root signatures do not use.
- **R2-0037** `docs/design/WINDOWS_BROKER_DESIGN.md:537` — Design mandates an empty-capability AppContainer and calls a restricted token non-equivalent; the plan allows a restricted token and the code builds only that *Evidence:* DESIGN :537 "this design **mandates AppContainer (lowbox)**", :549 restricted token has "no network confinement at all". PLAN :106 "Build via `CreateRestrictedToken` ... Preferred: an **AppContainer**". Code: only win-broker/src/bin/restricted_launch.rs:207 CreateRestrictedToken; no AppContainer AP… *Fix:* Add this to the design's 'superseded in part' list and state in the plan that a restricted token does not deliver the network denial §9/§10 assert.
- **R2-0038** `docs/roadmap/phase-6.md:78` — Phase 6 ticks 'Governed pack dispatch; per-builder verified receipts rendered' although no dispatch backend exists and nothing renders a receipt *Evidence:* phase-6.md:78 and :87 are [x]. agentsDispatch.ts:49-50 'Today no such backend command exists ... a real attempt resolves to `unreachable`'; :762 'Rejects in this build'. `dispatch_task_contract` is in no .rs/.py. grep -i receipt in agentsDispatch.ts, Agents.tsx, Command.tsx: 0 hits. *Fix:* Untick rows :78 and :87 (as Phases 8 and 9 did for the same missing engine capability) and correct the Phase 6 board fraction.
- **R2-0039** `engine/laws/registry.json:36` — Laws L1 and L8 name bro_completion.authorize_stop as their PRIMARY surface; that function never looks at a read receipt *Evidence:* registry l.36 and l.166: {module: bro_completion, symbol: authorize_stop, path_role: primary}. authorize_stop (bro_completion.py:1319-1328) only runs _authenticated_task/validate_completion/validate_verifier_receipt. Receipt freshness is enforced in bro_hook.py:123-138 and :179 via bro_policy.recei… *Fix:* Point L1 and L8 at the code that enforces them (bro_hook pre-tool/stop receipt check, bro_policy.receipt_fresh) instead of the completion gate.
- **R2-0040** `engine/runtime/challenge_authority.py:316` — PendingStore has no sweep and no quota; the design it cites as LOCKED requires both, and rows are never deleted *Evidence:* Addendum:1073-1079: a 60000 ms sweep 'deletes every PENDING row past pending_expires_at_ms and every ISSUED row past issued_at_ms + ISSUED_RETENTION_MS'; 'Quotas (fail-closed)' MAX_CONCURRENT_GOVERNED_TURNS / MAX_ISSUED_ROWS_PER_INSTALL=8 -> quota_pending. grep: no delete, no quota, no quota_pendin… *Fix:* Either implement the sweep and the two per-install quotas in PendingStore, or state in the module docstring that retention is logical only and quotas are NOT IMPLEMENTED.
- **R2-0041** `engine/runtime/governed_supervisor_server.py:603` — The broker's five-op path is still served beside the 4.10(d) driver, without the staging gate or execution allowlist the driver enforces *Evidence:* docs/OWNER_ACTION_REQUIRED.md:735 'the broker's direct-AF_UNIX GovernedChain is removed rather than kept beside it'. _op_accept_open :603-654 mints an acceptance and lease from a signed challenge alone; governed_acceptance.py:4 requires INPUTS_READY and :692 the execution_allowlist. run_ladder_supe… *Fix:* Either remove the broker op surface from the ladder supervisor, or make accept-open go through the same staging and allowlist gates as governed_acceptance.
- **R2-0042** `engine/runtime/supervisor_ledger.sql:399` — A staging session can be declared ARTIFACT_READY with zero chunks and any published_handle; the DDL says a bypassing writer cannot fabricate a finished upload *Evidence:* :313-319 'a writer that bypasses the handlers entirely still cannot fabricate a finished upload ... nothing can declare an ARTIFACT_READY session having uploaded nothing'. In sqlite: INSERT empty UPLOADING row, then `UPDATE ... SET state='ARTIFACT_READY', published_handle='x'` succeeded with next_s… *Fix:* Extend the session transition trigger so ARTIFACT_READY requires byte_count = declared_len and published_handle = declared_sha256, or narrow the comment to what the insert trigger actually guards.
- **R2-0043** `engine/schemas/control-room-command.schema.json:84` — Schema says an owner-signed command carries artifact_type/key_id/signature; the API refuses any command that carries them, and the artifact it does verify does not fit this schema *Evidence:* Schema :84-97 'Present only on a command the OWNER signed', signature 'over the JCS-canonical payload'. bro_control_room_api.py:906-908: `set(command) != required` (12 keys, none of the three) -> 'command fields do not match canonical schema'. The verified artifact payload carries role/agent_id/ses… *Fix:* Remove the three optional properties from the command schema and describe the separate signed attestation (its own schema), or make validate_command_intent accept them; keep one of the two shapes.
- **R2-0044** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:132` — Supervisor and runtime validate the same owner approval with different required fields; the approval has no expiry or single-use binding *Evidence:* authorize_request requires task_id, owner_approval, protected_scope. bro_protected.authorize_protected_scope:343 requires `task_class` in TASK_CLASSES and verification.independence_level. The test approval (test_supervisor.py:118) has neither. Nothing checks an expiry; task_id is chosen by the requ… *Fix:* Validate the approval in one shared function used by both sides, and require an expiry plus a one-time nonce in the approval payload.
- **R2-0045** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:679` — On timeout an uncontained process group is reported EXPIRED and its worktree is removed, contradicting the UNCONTAINED rule 4 lines below *Evidence:* `if timed_out: remove_worktree(...); return ... EXPIRED` runs before `if not contained:` whose comment says 'never COMPLETED, and the worktree is left in place rather than pulled out from under a process still using it'. test_supervisor.py:461 asserts EXPIRED for a timed-out group reported unstoppe… *Fix:* Check `not contained` first so a timeout with a live orphan returns UNCONTAINED and keeps the worktree.
- **R2-0046** `tools/check_coordination.py:95` — Status token says the governed round-trip is complete while the Phase-1 DoD row is open, and the gate refuses a state doc that says so *Evidence:* current_state.json:35 `CURRENT_GOVERNED_ROUNDTRIP: complete` (restated in 3 canon docs). docs/roadmap/phase-1.md:103 `- [ ] One governed round-trip proven end-to-end. **Still open`. SUBJECT_PROSE regex matched my probe sentence '...round-trip ... is still open' -> RED. Roadmap §J:454 says boxes mus… *Fix:* Set the token to the value the DoD row supports (or rename it to what is actually complete, the live-kit round-trip) and make the gate compare the token with the roadmap box.

### bug (13)

- **R2-0004** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:833` — The declare command the hook prints cannot work from the Bash tool: it carries no --session and the session id is never shown *Evidence:* Ran the printed form `python3 tools/check_roadmap_order.py --declare meta --note ...`: 'RED: cannot declare a phase: no full-read receipt for this session'. session_id() falls back to 'unknown'; `env | grep -c CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` = 0 in Bash. Header and status line never print sid. *Fix:* Interpolate `--session <sid>` into every command the hook and verify_declaration print.
- **R2-0005** `engine/.claude/settings.json:40` — Post-tool settlement is wired for shell tools only, but the pre-tool hook prepares a journal and reserves a lease for Write/Edit too *Evidence:* settings.json:40 and :51 matcher "Bash|PowerShell|Shell". PreToolUse is "*"; bro_control_plane.py:231-236 runs prepare_mutation + reserve_execution_lease for any mutating tool; tools/registry.json gives Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit WRITE_REPOSITORY. PREPARED is in bro_recovery.BLOCKING (:17).… *Fix:* Widen the PostToolUse and PostToolUseFailure matchers to every tool the pre-tool hook can reserve a lease for (or "*"), and add a wiring test that holds the two matchers together.
- **R2-0006** `engine/runtime/bro_contracts.py:88` — _require_string validates the stripped value but the contract keeps the padded one, so the validator accepts what the schema rejects *Evidence:* Ran _require_string(' abc-1 ','task_id',pattern=ID_RE) -> 'abc-1'. validate_task_contract checks `mode`/`risk` stripped (:275-280) and returns `value` unchanged; task-contract.schema.json has enum/pattern that reject padding. The path helpers at :119-146 were written to fix exactly this class. *Fix:* Refuse leading/trailing whitespace in _require_string (as _require_path_string does) instead of stripping, and extend ContractSchemaAgreementTests to the id/enum fields.
- **R2-0007** `engine/runtime/provision_floor_writer.py:482` — Root provisioner silently chowns and chmods an EXISTING socket directory, while its own comment forbids exactly that for the marks root *Evidence:* `_mkdir_owned(plan.socket_dir, ...)` is mkdir(exist_ok=True)+chown+chmod; only socket_dir.parent is custody-checked. `--socket-path /run/x.sock` re-owns /run. Reproduced in scratchpad: a 1777 dir holding another file came back 2750. :473 says an existing root is 'checked, never silently re-owned'. *Fix:* If socket_dir (or marks_dir) already exists, require it to be already owned by the service uid and empty (or refuse), exactly as the existing marks root is checked instead of re-owned.
- **R2-0008** `engine/tests/test_governed_output_read.py:837` — A 'refused' complete-run still records the completion: the attempt is COMPLETED with no output stream, and the two tests named 'refuses the completion' never look at the ledger *Evidence:* Probe of both cases (unreadable store, 8388609-byte output): reply ok=False reason=malformed_state, but governed_turn_completion has 1 row and acceptance state is COMPLETED, stream None. record_completion runs at governed_supervisor_server.py:803, measure/mint after it at :821-836. *Fix:* Measure the output and validate its size before record_completion (or in the same transaction), and make both tests assert the acceptance row is not COMPLETED after the refusal.
- **R2-0009** `engine/tools/bro_bind_workspace.py:70` — The workspace-binding tool cannot issue a binding for this monorepo with either root *Evidence:* Ran read-only: `--root . --print-digest-only` -> traceback `ProtectedScopeError: cannot read protected manifest: /home/gevorg/os/config/protected-control-plane.json`; detect_remote(engine) -> `WorkspaceError: /home/gevorg/os/engine is not a git checkout`. Manifest lives in engine/, .git at the repo… *Fix:* Take the git checkout root and the engine (control-plane) root as two separate arguments instead of one --root.
- **R2-0010** `engine/tools/bro_run_receipt.py:99` — The receipt runner cannot produce a receipt in the monorepo layout for either root *Evidence:* --root engine: tree identity is `git ls-files` with cwd=engine (326 files) vs the snapshot worktree of the whole repo (1432) -> `snapshot tree diverges`. --root repo: `catalog_sha256(snap)` reads snap/tests/catalog.json; /home/gevorg/os/tests/catalog.json does not exist (it is engine/tests/catalog.… *Fix:* Resolve the git toplevel separately from the engine root and compute tree identity and catalog path against the right one of the two.
- **R2-0011** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:443` — The supervised builder's environment carries no operator-root pin, so a builder running the wall cannot verify the lease it is handed *Evidence:* spawn_builder env = PATH, SYSTEMROOT, BRO_MODE, BRO_ROLE, BRO_AGENT_ID, BRO_SESSION_ID, BRO_EXECUTION_LEASE, BRO_WORKSPACE_BINDING, BRO_SESSION_STATE_DIR. bro_signature.py:570 raises `no operator-root pin` when neither BRO_OPERATOR_ROOT_PUBKEY_FILE nor BRO_OPERATOR_ROOT_PUBKEY is set. Tests verify… *Fix:* Pass the pin-file variable and BRO_TRUSTED_REGISTRY_ROOT (from --registry-root) into the builder env, and add a test whose builder actually runs bro_hook.
- **R2-0012** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:451` — The builder is given the operator binding unchanged while it runs in a temp worktree outside the binding's root *Evidence:* prepare_worktree: `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f"bro-wt-{task_id}-")`; env `BRO_WORKSPACE_BINDING: str(binding_path)`. bro_workspace.py:316 `if not _contained(workspace.root, resolved): raise WorkspaceError("path escapes workspace")` with workspace.root = binding["root"] (the main checkout). *Fix:* Create the worktree under the bound root, or have the supervisor obtain a binding whose root is the worktree before spawning.
- **R2-0013** `tools/check_handoff_ready.py:294` — The handoff gate never reads main's CI, though the law it quotes says 'verify the exact GitHub HEAD/CI' *Evidence:* Docstring :7 `verify the exact GitHub HEAD/CI`. main() runs six checks: canon, tree, pushed, handoff text, mirror commit exists, phase. No `gh`, no `main_ci` anywhere in the file (grep: one unrelated hit). It printed GREEN here without consulting any run. *Fix:* Add a seventh question that reuses check_repo_state.main_ci_failures, RED when main's newest completed run is not success or unreadable.
- **R2-0014** `tools/check_prior_art.py:112` — Rule 4 (gate input overlap) can never fire on the Edit/Write path: it reads the new file, which does not exist yet *Evidence:* gate_overlaps: `new_inputs = gate_inputs(root / rel); if not new_inputs: return {}`. Hook calls verify only `if rel and not (ROOT / rel).exists()` (canonical_law_gate.py:1311). Measured: gate_overlaps(root,'tools/check_zzz_new.py') -> {}; an existing gate -> 5 overlaps. *Fix:* Pass the Write tool's proposed content to gate_overlaps at PreToolUse instead of reading the not-yet-existing file.
- **R2-0015** `tools/check_release_signing.py:347` — Rule 5 (release.yml runs the preflight) is satisfied by the workflow's own header comment *Evidence:* release.yml:8 is a comment containing `tools/check_release_signing.py --require-release-ready`. Measured on a scratch copy: the step at :71 replaced by `run: echo skipped` -> check() returned `([], 'UNPROVISIONED')`, still GREEN. Same defect G-08 fixed for tests in check_reachability. *Fix:* Match the invocation only on a non-comment `run:` line inside the `preflight` job, and add a test that deletes the step and expects RED.
- **R2-0016** `tools/githooks/pre-commit:6` — The local half of the canonical update law cannot run: the hook is not executable and core.hooksPath is unset *Evidence:* `git ls-files -s` shows mode 100644; `git config core.hooksPath` prints nothing here. Reproduced in a scratch repo with the documented install command: "hint: The 'tools/githooks/pre-commit' hook was ignored because it's not set as executable" and the commit landed. *Fix:* Commit the hook as 100755 (`git update-index --chmod=+x`) and have a gate assert both the mode and that core.hooksPath is set, or stop claiming a local refusal.

### test-defect (27)

- **R2-0082** `bridge/tests/test_sidecar_ops.py:173` — ExecutionIsolationTests trips the dead door and leaves the live one unwatched: a read op that reaches the supervisor socket passes *Evidence:* _trip_wires replaces only _real_callables (raises unconditionally) and run_governed_turn. engine_sidecar.py:22-23 says no op may reach 'the supervisor socket'. Probe: governance.read handler patched in memory to call _supervisor_request -> 3 tests run, 0 fail, 3 supervisor attempts. *Fix:* Add _supervisor_request and _supervisor_socket_path to the trip wires, and drive approval_request() through them as well as read_request().
- **R2-0083** `bridge/tests/test_sidecar_ops.py:483` — The fake engine ignores the governance_read request, so the surface the approval hop asks for is tested by nothing here *Evidence:* 'def governance_read(self, _request, **_kwargs): return {"ok": True, "records": list(records)}'. Probe: changed '"surface": "evidenceChain"' to '"decisionLedger"' in _op_approval_request in memory -> 14 approval tests run, 0 fail. Class docstring claims the public surfaces are held. *Fix:* Have the fake record the request it receives and assert protocol, op, surface == 'evidenceChain', task_id and read_only.
- **R2-0084** `bridge/tests/test_task_request_contract.py:96` — test_a_tampered_hash_claim_is_caught_by_recompute exercises no code that catches anything; its key assertion is a tautology *Evidence:* Line 102 pins recomputed == 'e08caf22...'; line 103 then asserts '00'*32 != recomputed, which cannot fail. Lines 107-108 only show request_sha256 changes when an input changes. No signer or sidecar is called; _real_callables raises, so no task-request path recomputes anything. *Fix:* Either drive a real consumer (e.g. brops_sign_flow) with a tampered claim and assert the refusal, or rename the test to what it pins: the digest of the raw system string and request_sha256's sensitiv…
- **R2-0085** `engine/ci/live/ladder_evidence.py:511` — check_pull's range check trusts the driver's own expected_chunks, and passes vacuously when the field is absent (reproduced) *Evidence:* 'expected_chunks = int(positive.get("expected_chunks", 0))'. Ran check_pull with a positive document lacking the field, output_bytes=300000 and one ok hop with seq 7: it returned expected_chunks=0, served_seqs=[]. The docstring lists the ranges among 'five things the driver could not have fabricate… *Fix:* Derive the chunk count in the verifier from the signed envelope's output_bytes and the published chunk size, and refuse a positive document whose expected_chunks disagrees.
- **R2-0086** `engine/laws/registry.json:151` — L7's bound tests never call authorize_release_push or validate_exact_push *Evidence:* Surfaces l.147-148: bro_release_v3.authorize_release_push (primary), bro_security.validate_exact_push. Bound tests (test_release_v3.py:50,57) call only validate_release_grant_v3 through a patched helper; neither symbol appears anywhere in test_release_v3.py. *Fix:* Bind L7 to tests that call authorize_release_push (allow and deny) or name validate_release_grant_v3 as the surface the tests prove.
- **R2-0087** `engine/tests/test_live_broker_config.py:595` — 'No desktop crate sets BROPS_BROKER_CONFIG' is checked only for the literal set_var form *Evidence:* `re.search(r'set_var\s*\(\s*"BROPS_BROKER_CONFIG"', source)`. A `Command::new(..).env("BROPS_BROKER_CONFIG", ..)` or a set_var through a const passes. The `.env("BROPS_BROKER_CONFIG", "/cfg.json")` form is already in the tree (broker/src/preflight.rs:1798, a test host) and is not seen by this scan. *Fix:* Flag every occurrence of the variable name outside an allowlist of reader/test files rather than one call spelling.
- **R2-0088** `engine/tests/test_live_tcb_pin_manifest.py:96` — Tests write a fixed shared path /tmp/source-tree and fail when two suite runs overlap *Evidence:* source = join(dirname(normpath(root)), 'source-tree') with root a TemporaryDirectory, i.e. /tmp/source-tree; it exists on this box dated Sep 29 and is never removed. With 3-5 concurrent suite runs 1-3 tests of this module failed (different ones each run); serially 0. *Fix:* Create the deployment root one level down (tmp/root and tmp/source-tree inside the same TemporaryDirectory) so the sibling is private to the test.
- **R2-0089** `engine/tests/test_recovery.py:71` — validate_prepared_record's five binding checks are tested by nothing: prepare() always patches _signed_record with a matching record *Evidence:* Deleting any of bro_recovery.py:147 (shape/phase), 149 (effect class), 153 (task/agent/session/tool/action_hash/before-state mismatch), 155 (after-state present), 165 (capability/target mismatch) leaves the full engine suite at 2620 OK. No test file contains any of the five messages. *Fix:* Add one negative case per check that feeds a signed record differing in exactly that field and asserts the message.
- **R2-0090** `engine/tests/test_release_v3.py:82` — The release push path itself (authorize_release_push / settle_release_push) is called by no test *Evidence:* grep over engine/tests finds no reference to authorize_release_push, settle_release_push or _remote_head; only the parts are tested. Deleting bro_release_v3.py:151 (boundary gate) or :106 (timestamp types) leaves the full suite 2620 OK. L-law cites this file as the release allow/deny tests. *Fix:* Drive authorize_release_push and settle_release_push end to end with a signed grant, including the failed-push reconcile, quarantine and release branches.
- **R2-0091** `engine/tests/test_repository_state.py:74` — Every negative test of the repository binding passes with its check deleted (main-mutation denial included) *Evidence:* Mutated scratch copies of bro_repository_state.py, one check removed each (lines 163,171,175,177,180,182: worktree, registered, main/master, branch, HEAD, tree). All 8 tests here stayed OK, and the full engine suite stayed 2620 OK for each. Unpatched _load_lock raises the same RepositoryStateError. *Fix:* Patch _load_lock with a valid lock in every negative case and assert the refusal message (assertRaisesRegex), so each check is the only possible cause.
- **R2-0092** `engine/tests/test_signature_authority.py:330` — Production binding, raw-pin CI gate and the registry anti-rollback floor have no engine test *Evidence:* Full suite stays 2620 OK with each deleted: bro_signature.py:933 (dev registry under FILE pin refused), 561 (BRO_OPERATOR_ROOT_PUBKEY only in CI), 638 (raw floor only in CI), 944 (sha floor), 951, 955 (rolled-back registry), 967 (duplicate key id). grep: no engine test sets BRO_OPERATOR_REGISTRY_MI… *Fix:* Add load_trusted_keys cases: file-pinned non-production registry, raw pin without BRO_ENV=ci, floor above the registry version, sha floor mismatch, duplicate key_id.
- **R2-0093** `engine/tests/test_workspace_scope.py:395` — load_workspace's schema, required-field and root-exists refusals, and two normalize_remote refusals, are untested *Evidence:* Full suite stays 2620 OK with bro_workspace.py:178 (schema != 1), :183 (missing workspace_id/repository/root/allowed_remote_repository), :197 (root not a directory), :110 (unrecognized URL), :116 (not owner/repo) deleted. test_supervisor's schema case tests the supervisor's own check, not this one. *Fix:* Add BindingLoadTests cases for schema=2, each omitted field and a nonexistent root, and RemoteTests cases for a non-URL and a three-segment path.
- **R2-0094** `tools/check_repo_state.py:703` — The live branch-protection comparison (H-04) is tested by nothing and, by its own docstring, never runs in CI *Evidence:* Replaced verify_branch_protection with `return []` for any readable `live`: 224 tests (test_check_repo_state, test_check_main_ci, test_sync_active_pr, test_check_control_invocation) stay green. Only test call is test_check_repo_state.py:883 with live=None. Docstring: in CI it is a 403 SKIP. *Fix:* Add fixture tests driving verify_branch_protection with a live dict: each flag flipped, strict flipped, a missing context and an extra context must each be RED.
- **R2-0095** `tools/test_check_capabilities.py:612` — The native-dialog marker test loops over the gate's own HANDLER_MARKERS, so removing a marker from the gate is invisible *Evidence:* `for marker in cc.HANDLER_MARKERS:` (:613). Dropping each of the three ('window: tauri::Window', 'ConfirmationGuard::acquire()?', '.blocking_show()') from check_capabilities.py:91 left 45 tests green, including the real-handler test. *Fix:* Write the three markers literally in the test and assert the gate's tuple equals them.
- **R2-0096** `tools/test_check_contrast.py:138` — Nothing tests that main() calls the palette-mirror check; the mutant that deletes the call is green *Evidence:* `if args.manifest == DEFAULT_MANIFEST:` -> `if False:` at check_contrast.py:368 left the whole module green. Both main() tests (:138, :145) pass a temp manifest, the one spelling for which the check is skipped; the mirror tests call the helper directly. *Fix:* Add a main() test on a synthetic root whose manifest disagrees with its tokens.ts and require exit 1.
- **R2-0097** `tools/test_check_crypto_surface.py:206` — 16 of 18 forbidden key loaders and 3 of 5 forbidden modules (pkcs12, cms, ocsp) are held by no test; the from-import arm is untested *Evidence:* Element-drop on check_crypto_surface.py:105/:120: 16/18 FORBIDDEN_LOADERS and 3/5 FORBIDDEN_MODULES survive all tests. `if segment in parts:` (:217, `from cryptography.<module> import`) and the bare-name arm (:248) survive `if False`. *Fix:* Parametrise one refused-import test over every entry of both tables written out literally in the test, and add a `from cryptography.x509 import X` case.
- **R2-0098** `tools/test_check_handoff_ready.py:231` — The no-upstream test is red for six reasons and asserts only the exit code; the no-upstream arm can be deleted *Evidence:* The `solo` fixture prints 6 problems (over budget, no upstream, head, branch, next action, no current_state.json). `if code != 0:` -> `if False:` at check_handoff_ready.py:171 left all tests green. The file's own docstrings (:123-126, :189-191) name this exact defect. *Fix:* Build the fixture from build_repo() with the upstream removed and assert on the 'has no upstream' message.
- **R2-0099** `tools/test_check_ledger_ddl_parity.py:27` — The ledger DDL enforcement floor is tested against a copy of itself: all 55 clauses can be deleted from the gate with the suite green *Evidence:* GOOD is built from REQUIRED_CLAUSES (:27-30) and the removal test iterates the same tuple (:72). Measured: dropping each of the 55 elements of check_ledger_ddl_parity.py:39 in a scratch copy left `Ran 10 tests OK` 55 times of 55. *Fix:* Pin the floor in the test with a literal list (or count plus the named triggers/UNIQUEs) so shrinking REQUIRED_CLAUSES turns a named test red.
- **R2-0100** `tools/test_check_no_owner_key_ceremony.py:308` — The phrase list is pinned by set-equality, the token and path lists are not: 11 of 14 forbidden tokens and 3 of 4 removed paths can be deleted unseen *Evidence:* Dropping each element with the 27 tests run after: FORBIDDEN_TOKENS 11/14 survive (e.g. '[a phrase gate-refused since #314]', '[a phrase gate-refused since #314]', '[a phrase gate-refused since #314]'), REMOVED_PATHS 3/4, TEXT_EXT 12/23 ('.json', '.yml', '.sh', '.toml'), EXEMPT_PREFIXES 3/6. *Fix:* Add the same both-directions table test for FORBIDDEN_TOKENS, REMOVED_PATHS and TEXT_EXT that `test_every_phrase_has_the_sentence_it_was_written_for` gives the phrases.
- **R2-0101** `tools/test_check_produced_artifact.py:335` — A committed receipts log is refused by the gate and by no test; 36 of 75 checks survive deletion *Evidence:* Sweep of check_produced_artifact.py: L553 `if _git_tracked(root, receipts_rel)` -> False, 37 tests green (c1 store and c4 run log have this test, c5 does not). Also surviving: L285 bool-vs-integer, L432 grant path outside the artifact, L310/367/412/487/508/514/542/563 undeclared contract keys. *Fix:* Add test_c5_refuses_a_committed_receipt_log mirroring test_c4_refuses_a_committed_run_log, and one fixture per undeclared key and for a grant ref escaping the artifact.
- **R2-0102** `tools/test_check_release_signing.py:461` — The release-workflow rules `preflight:` job, `needs: preflight` and `continue-on-error` have no test of their own *Evidence:* Sweep: check_release_signing.py L353, L355, L360, L366 replaced by `if False` -> 45 tests green. The one test asserts any('preflight' in p), which the first message ('...without the preflight the workflow can publish...') already satisfies. The module docstring lists this case as covered. *Fix:* Add three fixtures - step present but no preflight job, job present but build lacks needs:, continue-on-error: true - each asserting its own message.
- **R2-0103** `tools/test_check_repo_state.py:109` — main()'s RED exit and several documented refusals in check_repo_state are untested *Evidence:* Sweep (`if False`, 120 tests green): L1265 `if failures:` (the gate's only RED exit past the no-gh path), L213 event base != snapshot base (docstring line 4 promises 'event base/head/number mismatch must be RED'), L202 non-hex base sha, L279 pre_merge.carrier_state, L433/L435 verify_main_push sha c… *Fix:* Add PrEventTests for a base-ref mismatch and a non-sha base, a pre_merge carrier_state test, and one main() test with gh stubbed that asserts exit 1 on a failing snapshot.
- **R2-0104** `tools/test_check_roadmap_order.py:52` — The ninth-audit I-07 fraction rule has no test; 15 of 35 checks in check_roadmap_order survive deletion *Evidence:* `grep fraction tools/test_*.py` finds no roadmap test. Sweep: L202 `if printed != counted` -> False leaves 26 tests green; also L116 (no Definition-of-Done block), L217, L233 (orphan board rows), L341 (non-integer declared phase), L410 (main's RED exit). *Fix:* Add fixtures: a board row printing 8/10 over a section counting 7/9, a phase with no DoD block, an orphan board row; assert each message and main()'s exit code.
- **R2-0105** `tools/test_check_schema_mirrors.py:180` — test_strip_test_modules_brace_counts_rather_than_stopping_at_the_first_brace passes when the stripper stops at the first brace *Evidence:* Swapped strip_test_modules for one that cuts at the first `}`: the named test and all 30 tests in the module stay green. The fixture's test module is the last thing in SRC and the asserted name sits before the first `}`. Sweep: L130, L132, L134 `if False` all survive. *Fix:* Give the fixture a second nested fn in `mod tests` that mentions get("schema") plus a production fn after the module, and assert the first is gone and the second kept.
- **R2-0106** `tools/test_check_schema_mirrors.py:233` — check_schema_mirrors.main()'s six failure arms are driven by no fixture *Evidence:* Sweep: L283 (schema missing), L286 (rust file missing), L292 (struct not found - the arm test :73 says 'the caller turns this into a FAILURE'), L308, L313 (negative test missing), L318 (no parser), L323 (`if failures`) all survive `if False`; the only main() test runs the real, green repository. *Fix:* Patch m.MIRRORS and --root to a temp tree and assert main() returns 1 with the right message for each arm.
- **R2-0107** `tools/test_check_version_parity.py:160` — The test names a mutant it cannot kill: a regex anchored to `version` alone still passes *Evidence:* Docstring: 'Mutant: anchor the regex to `version` alone instead of the [package] table => the gate reads "2"'. Measured in memory: _CARGO_PACKAGE_VERSION = r'^version\s*=...' -> this test 0 failures and the whole module 0 failures. The fixture puts [package] before the trap. *Fix:* Put a `[workspace.package] version = "9.9.9"` (or the tauri-build table) BEFORE [package] in the fixture so a first-match regex reads the wrong value.
- **R2-0108** `tools/test_sync_active_pr.py:401` — Both 'shape guards' in sync_active_pr are untested, though the test docstring credits one with catching a real defect *Evidence:* Sweep: sync_active_pr.py L509 `if before_keys != after_keys` and L787 (main_ci shape) -> False, 75 tests green. NoteSurgeryTests docstring: 'the shape guard doing its job'. The two test_a_writer_that_refuses_midway tests replace the writer with a stub that raises. L180 sha check and L403 also survi… *Fix:* Feed rewrite_state and rewrite_main_ci a snapshot whose note scan ends early (e.g. a note key in a layout the scan misreads) and assert the SHAPE refusal and an unchanged file.

- **R2-0017** `engine/runtime/bro_control_plane.py:13` — Session freeze is never created in production: freeze_authority is imported and called by nothing but one test *Evidence:* grep freeze_authority across engine/bridge: def in bro_freeze.py:60, this unused import, tests/test_completion_gate.py:224. So load_freeze() at :179 and bro_completion.py:1365 ('refuses a frozen session') can never return a freeze; the :183 comment describes a mutation-then-freeze flow nothing perf… *Fix:* Either call freeze_authority where a protected-scope mutation settles, or delete the freeze branch and the docstring claims that the conductor exemption refuses a frozen session.

### security (18)

- **R2-0050** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:366` — Meta scope, canon budget and shell settlement do not apply inside .claude/worktrees/, where agents edit product code *Evidence:* Ran scope_problem for a meta receipt: 'apps/desktop/src/App.tsx' => refused; '.claude/worktrees/agent-x/apps/desktop/src/App.tsx' => None (rel starts with META prefix '.claude/'). ceilings.get(rel) never matches a worktree copy; .gitignore:44 hides worktrees from git status. *Fix:* Strip a leading `.claude/worktrees/<id>/` from the relative path before the scope, prior-art and budget predicates run.
- **R2-0051** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:1005` — The T-150 recogniser misses `git push` behind a wrapper option that takes a value, a leading redirect, or xargs - and none of these is in its 'does not cover' list *Evidence:* Ran guarded_invocations(): 'nice -n 10 git push', 'sudo -u gevorg git push', 'env -u FOO git push', 'timeout -s KILL 30 git push', '>/dev/null git push', 'xargs git push', 'git push>log' all return []. _command_words skips only words starting with '-', so the option's value becomes the program name. *Fix:* After stripping wrappers, scan every remaining word position for a `git ... push` / `gh ... pr merge` head (as reads_as_guarded already does) instead of trusting words[0], and list what is still unco…
- **R2-0052** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:1044` — A merge through the REST API (`gh api -X PUT .../pulls/N/merge`) is not recognised and is not in the 'WHAT IT DOES NOT COVER' list *Evidence:* Ran: guarded_invocations('gh api -X PUT repos/menqstudio/OS/pulls/5/merge') -> [] (positional[:2] != ['pr','merge']); reads_as_guarded -> False. Docstring lines 115-124 list web UI, scripts, eval, other repos - not gh api. This repo's own memory says 'gh pr edit is broken here - use REST'. *Fix:* Recognise `gh api` whose path matches pulls/<n>/merge with a PUT method as a merge, or name it in the not-covered list and in config/control-invocation.json.
- **R2-0053** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:1082` — A REST merge (`gh api -X PUT .../pulls/N/merge`) passes the merge gate and is not in the hook's own 'does not cover' list *Evidence:* Ran guarded_invocations('gh api -X PUT repos/o/r/pulls/5/merge') -> [] although mentions_guarded is True. _gh_merge requires positionals ['pr','merge']. The WHAT IT DOES NOT COVER list (lines 115-125) names scripts, eval, sh -c, other repos; not gh api. Project memory says REST is the working road… *Fix:* Treat `gh api` whose path matches pulls/<N>/merge as a merge of N, or list it by name under WHAT IT DOES NOT COVER.
- **R2-0054** `docs/OPERATOR_GUIDE.md:342` — Operator and user guides say agent-mode Bash is bounded by a deny list; the list is empty and the code says the shell is not bounded *Evidence:* OPERATOR_GUIDE:342 'Bash is bounded by a deny list — no delete, no git push, no dependency install, no nested shell'; :373 and USER_GUIDE:210 'a bounded shell'. ai.rs:1203 `const BRO_BASH_DENY: &[&str] = &[];`, ai.rs:1847 'The shell is not bounded.' SECURITY.md:33 'empty by Owner decision, 2026-08-… *Fix:* Replace the three guide sentences with SECURITY.md's wording (shell unbounded by Owner decision; only trust-surface writes and built-in agent types are refused) and keep SECURITY.md as the one copy.
- **R2-0055** `engine/agents/authority-policy.json:3` — Verifier authority is derived from packs/registry.json, which is neither protected nor digested, and the identity fingerprint does not cover the flag that decides it *Evidence:* authority-policy.json:3 "source": "packs/registry.json". bro_authority.py:75 designates roles[-1] where independent_verifier_required is True. protected-control-plane.json roots list agents/authority-policy.json and agents/registry.json, no packs/**. bro_identity.py:52 hashes only pack_id+roles. *Fix:* On an audited engine branch, add packs/registry.json to protected_roots and digest_roots (or include independent_verifier_required in the pinned fingerprint).
- **R2-0056** `engine/config/protected-control-plane.json:4` — Five registered SSTs sit outside protected_roots and digest_roots, including packs/registry.json which authority derivation reads *Evidence:* Not matched by any protected root: packs/registry.json, skills/index.json, analytics/registry.json, learning/registry.json, release/registry.json. bro_authority.py:75-79 designates verifiers from packs' independent_verifier_required and roles[-1]; the identity fingerprint covers ids and roles only,… *Fix:* Add the five SST paths (at least packs/registry.json and skills/index.json) to both lists, or name them in the 'NOT covered' sentence as an owner decision.
- **R2-0057** `engine/runtime/bro_completion.py:193` — "Must be outside the repository" is checked against engine/, so a path inside the OS git checkout passes *Evidence:* ROOT = parents[1] = /home/gevorg/os/engine. Ran: BRO_EVIDENCE_STORE=/home/gevorg/os/docs/x -> _external_dir returns it; bro_audit_log._assert_external('/home/gevorg/os/docs/ledger.jsonl', ROOT) returns None. Same test in bro_contracts.py:515 and callers passing repo_root=ROOT. *Fix:* Resolve the repository as the git top-level (or the highest ancestor holding .git) once, in one shared helper, and test every external store against that.
- **R2-0058** `engine/runtime/bro_security.py:382` — PowerShell cmdlets are allowlisted as read-only but the parser models POSIX quoting only *Evidence:* classify_tool_action('PowerShell', 'echo (Remove-Item x)') -> READ_LOCAL, mutating=False, targets=(); so is 'echo "a\\" ; Remove-Item x ; echo "b\\"' (backslash is not an escape in PowerShell). Classification only: no pwsh on this box to execute it. *Fix:* For PowerShell refuse unquoted `(`, `@(`, `{` and treat backslash as a literal, or stop classifying any PowerShell segment as read-only.
- **R2-0059** `engine/runtime/bro_workspace.py:286` — verify_repository_binding misses a key written on the section-header line, so origin can point anywhere *Evidence:* Scratch repo with `[remote "origin"] url = https://github.com/attacker/repo` plus a decoy remote on its own lines: `git config --get remote.origin.url` returned the attacker url and verify_repository_binding PASSED. The regexes anchor url/pushurl/insteadOf at line start (`^\s*url\s*=`). *Fix:* Parse the file with a real git-config parser (or `git config --file <path> --list`) instead of line-anchored regexes, or refuse any header line with trailing content.
- **R2-0060** `engine/runtime/isolated_signer_server.py:298` — The signer's front door parses with plain json.loads: duplicate keys and NaN are accepted, while NM-FRAME-02 is marked 'implemented' on a different parser *Evidence:* `request = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))`. Ran handle_connection with `{"op":"nope","op":"sign-result","sign_request":{"a":1,"a":NaN}}`: dispatched to the signer with {'a': nan}. NM-FRAME-02's test only calls brops_protocol.strict_loads. Design 1.9/4: duplicate keys rejected. *Fix:* Parse the frame body with brops_protocol.strict_loads in handle_connection (and the three sibling servers) and bind NM-FRAME-02 to a test that drives the real door.
- **R2-0061** `engine/tests/test_governed_supervisor_ledger.py:675` — The evidence-head floor accepts a replayed head for a NEW attempt in the same task, and this test pins it as 'idempotent' *Evidence:* Probe run: att-1 task-1 head 12 -> created; a different attempt att-2 (new nonce/challenge), same task, same DERIVED -> 'created' and load_attestation_state is not None. The same replay under another task is EvidenceFork (test :656). CAS runs only `if inserted`, so its 'idempotent' arm is only reac… *Fix:* Treat an equal head presented by a different execution_attempt_id as EvidenceFork (key the per-task idempotency on the attempt), and change this test to expect the refusal.
- **R2-0062** `engine/tools/broctl.py:162` — "Inside the repository" is tested against engine/, so key directories and ledgers elsewhere in the git repo pass as external *Evidence:* REPO_ROOT = parents[1] = /home/gevorg/os/engine; keygen refuses only paths under it. Ran `bro_deploy_preflight._external_absolute('/home/gevorg/os/ledger.jsonl', ROOT)` -> None (accepted); under engine/ -> 'must be outside the repository'. Root .gitignore ignores only `keys/`, `*.key`, `*.pem`. *Fix:* Derive the repository root from `git rev-parse --show-toplevel` (or walk up to .git) in one shared helper and use it in both tools.
- **R2-0063** `tools/check_ai_surfaces.py:54` — A command that calls an imported or aliased provider entry is invisible to the AI-surface inventory *Evidence:* _PROVIDER_RE requires the literal `ai::` prefix. Probed check(): `use crate::ai::{generate}; ... generate(&p, req)` in a #[tauri::command] -> [] ; `use crate::ai as model; model::generate(..)` -> []. Docstring: 'This gate makes that impossible to merge.' No such import exists in src today (grep: 0). *Fix:* Also resolve `use ...ai::{...}` / `use ...ai as X` imports per file and match the bare or aliased call, or refuse those import forms outright.
- **R2-0064** `tools/check_ai_surfaces.py:60` — A helper with a pub(super)/const/unsafe head is not a fn head, so its provider call can be attributed to an unrelated non-command fn and vanish *Evidence:* _FN_RE = `(?:pub(?:\(crate\))? )?(?:async )?fn`. Probe: `fn unrelated(){}` then `pub(super) async fn hidden(){ crate::ai::generate(..) }` then a #[tauri::command] calling hidden() -> check() returned [] (GREEN, unclassified). *Fix:* Match any visibility and qualifier before `fn` (pub(...), const, unsafe, extern) so every helper is its own resolvable head.
- **R2-0065** `tools/check_capabilities.py:169` — Capability gate ignores every permission that is not allow-*/deny-*, so a plugin or bundled permission in default.json is granted unseen *Evidence:* Probe: grants_from_permissions(['core:default','shell:allow-execute','fs:allow-write-file','allow-list-dir','my-bundle']) -> {'list_dir': 'allow'}. check() compares only that dict with the policy; nothing inventories the rest. default.json holds 3 such today (core:default, core:window:default, core… *Fix:* Hold the non-command identifiers in default.json to an explicit allowlist in the gate and go RED on any other.
- **R2-0066** `tools/check_read_receipt.py:192` — A receipt file holding non-object JSON crashes verify(), and the hook fails OPEN on any gate exception *Evidence:* Measured: receipt file containing `[]` -> `CRASH AttributeError 'list' object has no attribute 'get'`. load() catches only OSError/JSONDecodeError/ReceiptError; canonical_law_gate.py:1411 `except Exception` -> 'FAIL OPEN, LOUDLY' exit 0. One write to /tmp turns receipt, phase, scope, prior-art and… *Fix:* Make load() return None unless the parsed value is a dict, so a malformed receipt reads as 'no receipt' and is refused or refreshed instead of raising.
- **R2-0067** `tools/check_roadmap_order.py:382` — Meta scope lets a session edit product code through any .claude/worktrees/ path, and never normalises `..` *Evidence:* Measured with declared_phase=meta: `.claude/worktrees/agent-x/engine/runtime/bro_hook.py -> ALLOWED`, `tools/../engine/runtime/bro_hook.py -> ALLOWED`, `engine/runtime/bro_hook.py -> refused`. Line 382 is a bare `rel.startswith(META_ALLOWED_PREFIXES)`. *Fix:* Normalise the path (posixpath.normpath, refuse `..`), strip a leading `.claude/worktrees/<id>/` before applying the prefix test.
