### bug (157)

- **R2-0109** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:139` — A malformed CANONICAL_LAW_INJECT_BYTES switches the whole wall off without the FAILED OPEN notice *Fix:* Parse the variable inside main() under the existing fail-open handler, falling back to the default.
- **R2-0110** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:181` — GATE_TIMEOUT is per gate, so one command line with a push and a merge can run 100 s of gates - past the minute the constant says it must stay under *Fix:* Share one deadline across all invocations in a command line (or refuse a line that carries more than one guarded command).
- **R2-0111** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:612` — The dirty-tree parser does not unquote git's C-style path quoting, so a non-ASCII path is never fingerprinted or prior-art checked *Fix:* Call git status with `-z` and split on NUL.
- **R2-0112** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:654` — dirty_fingerprints parses `git status --porcelain` without -z, so a quoted non-ASCII or ' -> '-containing path is mis-read and a new such file skips the prior-art check *Fix:* Use `git status --porcelain -z` and split on NUL, taking the rename source from the following record.
- **R2-0113** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:1005` — The T-150 push/merge arm does not recognise a wrapper that takes a value flag, though its docstring says wrappers are dropped *Fix:* After a wrapper, keep skipping until a word whose basename is gh or git (or refuse when gh/git appears later in the same segment), and add these five…
- **R2-0114** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:1066` — `git push origin :` is classified as a delete and skips the push gate, but a bare `:` is git's 'push all matching branches' refspec *Fix:* Treat a ref as a delete only when it is ':' followed by a non-empty name, skip the value of -o/--push-option/--repo/--receive-pack, and add both case…
- **R2-0115** `.claude/hooks/canonical_law_gate.py:1416` — The fail-open shout for a crashed `subagent-start` is emitted under hookEventName 'SessionStart' *Fix:* Add 'subagent-start': 'SubagentStart' to the mapping.
- **R2-0116** `.claude/hooks/coordination_stop_guard.py:46` — The Stop guard does not see a new source file until it is staged *Fix:* Add `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard` to the three sources and a test with an untracked file.
- **R2-0117** `.gitattributes:10` — The extensionless Debian postinst script has no eol rule, the same gap the file closes for the git hook *Fix:* Add `apps/desktop/src-tauri/deb/* text eol=lf`.
- **R2-0118** `.github/workflows/release.yml:185` — Nothing ties the release tag to the app version: any v* tag names the release, whatever version the installers carry *Fix:* Have the preflight refuse unless GITHUB_REF_NAME equals 'v' + the tauri.conf.json version.
- **R2-0119** `bridge/engine_adapter.py:43` — Adapter can emit ok:true with receipt.task_id null, which its own result schema forbids, and never validates its output *Fix:* Fail closed when the outcome has no string task_id, or fall back to the request's task_id as the failure paths already do.
- **R2-0120** `bridge/engine_sidecar.py:213` — --self-test-signed leaks one temp directory per run *Fix:* Use tempfile.TemporaryDirectory bound to the callables' lifetime, or register an atexit cleanup.
- **R2-0121** `bridge/engine_sidecar.py:907` — approval.request discards the engine's reason when the evidence-chain read is refused, and reports a different one *Fix:* When the evidenceChain read is not ok, return _approval_refusal(request, read.get('error')) so the operator sees the real cause.
- **R2-0122** `config/counted-claims.json:197` — engine_tests 'superseded' stopped at 2314; the twelve counts recorded since are not refused in cited documents *Fix:* Have the gate derive superseded from every non-current `ran` value in `environments`, instead of a hand-kept list.
- **R2-0123** `docs/PHASE_10_PRODUCTION_ITEMS.md:272` — 'three-valued shadow.anchor.state' followed by four values *Fix:* Say four-valued.
- **R2-0124** `docs/RELEASE_SETUP.md:16` — Docs and gate claim an RFC-3161 timestamp; tauri.conf.json sets timestampUrl without tsp, which Tauri documents as not RFC 3161 *Fix:* Add "tsp": true to bundle.windows and make check_release_signing.py require it, or drop the RFC-3161 wording.
- **R2-0125** `docs/design/WAVE_3B1B_EXECUTION_BINDING_ADDENDUM.md:1469` — The frame-sizing proof claims at least 16 KiB of headroom; its own numbers give 16181 bytes *Fix:* Write the headroom as 16181 bytes.
- **R2-0126** `docs/roadmap/phase-3.md:87` — Three links in the phase files are root-relative and resolve under docs/roadmap/, where the targets do not exist *Fix:* Rewrite the three targets as ../OWNER_ACTION_REQUIRED.md, ../design/CONTRACTS_DEDUPE_PLAN.md and ../DESIGN_SYSTEM.md.
- **R2-0127** `engine/ci/isolation_proof.sh:93` — The isolation proof removes nothing it created; its sibling proves its own cleanup *Fix:* Adopt the floor-writer proof's cleanup trap, with fixed-prefix accounts removed on exit.
- **R2-0128** `engine/ci/isolation_proof.sh:93` — The isolation proof removes nothing it creates: two accounts, a group, a copy of the engine and a directory of private keys stay on the host *Fix:* Give isolation_proof.sh the floor-writer proof's cleanup: remove the two temp trees on every exit path and report what is still present.
- **R2-0129** `engine/ci/isolation_proof.sh:97` — Readiness is judged by the socket node existing, the flake the sibling proof documents and fixed *Fix:* Wait for a completed exchange (any well-formed answer) instead of the node, as start_service in the floor-writer proof does.
- **R2-0130** `engine/ci/live/ladder_evidence.py:177` — The manifest signature is decoded leniently here while the strict verifier written for exactly this sits unused in live_crypto (reproduced) *Fix:* Call lc.verify_b64std(root_pub, manifest_bytes, manifest_sig) and delete the inline decode; add a test with a non-canonical signature.
- **R2-0131** `engine/ci/live/ladder_evidence.py:308` — The read-only ledger fallback cannot fire for the case its comment describes, and when it does fire it opens read-write (reproduced) *Fix:* Open with 'file:...?mode=ro&immutable=0' and treat a failure at the first query as a named RED, or drop the fallback and its comment.
- **R2-0132** `engine/ci/live/run_ladder_supervisor.py:243` — The durably recorded process_group_id is sudo's pid, not a process group, and it is reported before any launcher confirmation *Fix:* Start the child in its own session and record that group id, or rename the field's content to what it is and record the gap against §5.
- **R2-0133** `engine/ci/live/run_ladder_turn.sh:1590` — The driver's challenge document is decoded with the lenient base64 the verifier it feeds calls wrong *Fix:* Decode with brops_protocol.decode_base64url from the staged runtime, as ladder_evidence.unb64u does.
- **R2-0134** `engine/ci/live/run_live_turn.sh:122` — Run as plain root (no SUDO_USER), the kit declares login uid 0 and the floor then treats root as an untrusted principal *Fix:* Refuse to start when SUDO_USER is unset or resolves to uid 0, with a message naming the reason.
- **R2-0135** `engine/ci/live/run_live_turn.sh:443` — The cleanup trap is armed 336 lines after the setuid-root launcher is installed; the header's 'On EVERY exit path' is false *Fix:* Define cleanup and arm the trap before the first install into $TCB (above line 105) in both kits, and correct the comment at 427-428.
- **R2-0136** `engine/ci/live/run_signer.py:123` — The socket is made connectable after bind(), while the kit's readiness test is the socket file existing *Fix:* Wait for each server's 'RESULT: ... listening' line (printed after the chmod), or probe a real connect, instead of testing -S.
- **R2-0137** `engine/install/brops_install.sh:372` — An unreadable passwd database in the only-human-account path is reported as 'holds no human account' *Fix:* Call passwd_enumerate in the main shell first (or add `|| exit 1` after the inner substitution / shopt -s inherit_errexit) so the FAILED exit propaga…
- **R2-0138** `engine/runtime/bro_audit_log.py:592` — append() on a ledger whose last line lacks hash/seq raises KeyError, which the control-plane audit wrapper does not catch *Fix:* Validate the tail record's shape in append() and raise AuditMalformed, so every caller that catches AuditError gets a verdict.
- **R2-0139** `engine/runtime/bro_audit_log.py:652` — The 'missing seq / prev_hash' arm of verify() is unreachable; a record without them is reported as a chain break *Fix:* Run the missing-key check before the seq and prev_hash comparisons.
- **R2-0140** `engine/runtime/bro_audit_log.py:689` — A non-UTF-8 head anchor escapes verify() as UnicodeDecodeError, not AuditError *Fix:* Catch ValueError (as the .head branch does) in both anchor reads.
- **R2-0141** `engine/runtime/bro_authority.py:159` — IdentityError is wrapped in one resolver and escapes from the two other entry points *Fix:* Wrap the two all_agent_identities calls the same way.
- **R2-0142** `engine/runtime/bro_completion.py:1084` — Epoch fields accept JSON booleans while head_sequence in the same function explicitly refuses them *Fix:* Use one shared 'strict int' predicate that excludes bool for every signed integer field.
- **R2-0143** `engine/runtime/bro_completion.py:1131` — evidence_event_ids is never type-checked, so a malformed signed manifest raises TypeError past the CompletionError handler *Fix:* Require a non-empty list of non-empty strings in _check_manifest and _check_verifier_receipt before the chain call.
- **R2-0144** `engine/runtime/bro_completion.py:1244` — authorize_completion_docs is annotated as returning a 2-tuple and returns three values *Fix:* Annotate as tuple[dict[str, Any], str, dict[str, Any]].
- **R2-0145** `engine/runtime/bro_contracts.py:491` — validate_mode_grant compares timestamps without checking their type or that expiry follows issue *Fix:* Add the integer check and `expires <= issued` refusal, as the skill receipt and lease validators do.
- **R2-0146** `engine/runtime/bro_control_room_api.py:623` — governance_read can still raise after promising a reply document *Fix:* Compute known_task inside the guarded block and return a refusal on failure.
- **R2-0147** `engine/runtime/bro_env_health.py:49` — satisfies() reads an unsupported operator as a prefix match, so '!=' means the opposite *Fix:* Raise EnvHealthError on a clause that starts with any operator character not in the supported list.
- **R2-0148** `engine/runtime/bro_env_health.py:112` — A dependency whose version cannot be read passes its constraint *Fix:* Treat an unreadable version as a failure when the entry declares a constraint, or state in the docstring and the record that the constraint was not c…
- **R2-0149** `engine/runtime/bro_execution_lease.py:72` — The egress duplicate check is textual, so one destination can be listed three ways *Fix:* Refuse leading-zero ports and normalise the default port before the duplicate check.
- **R2-0150** `engine/runtime/bro_execution_lease.py:192` — Lease integer fields accept JSON booleans *Fix:* Add `isinstance(x, bool)` refusals for the three fields.
- **R2-0151** `engine/runtime/bro_freeze.py:29` — BRO_SESSION_STATE_DIR is checked absolute only; the outside-the-repository rule its docstring and the preflight both state is not enforced at use *Fix:* Resolve the directory through the one shared external-path resolver (see the eight-copies finding) so an in-repo path is refused at use.
- **R2-0152** `engine/runtime/bro_hook.py:163` — Release settlement catches only ReleaseV3Error, but settle_release_push raises SecurityError and CompletionError too *Fix:* Wrap those calls inside settle_release_push and re-raise as ReleaseV3Error (bro_release_v3 already imports SecurityError and never uses it).
- **R2-0153** `engine/runtime/bro_hook.py:227` — fail_closed labels a failed subagent-start or prompt event as SessionStart *Fix:* Map the event to its real hook name in fail_closed.
- **R2-0154** `engine/runtime/bro_learning.py:28` — A learning registry that is not a JSON object raises AttributeError instead of LearningError *Fix:* Raise LearningError unless value is a dict.
- **R2-0155** `engine/runtime/bro_orchestration_runtime.py:626` — The stale-lock branch of _claim_guard loops with no deadline check and no sleep *Fix:* Check the deadline and sleep before `continue` in the stale branch.
- **R2-0156** `engine/runtime/bro_orchestration_runtime.py:856` — A budget-blocked task re-blocks on its first usage record after an approved retry *Fix:* Have retry-approved carry a raised limit or a usage baseline that record_usage reads.
- **R2-0157** `engine/runtime/bro_orchestration_runtime.py:887` — retry_blocked and cancel_task mutate outside the claim guard every other mutating entry point holds *Fix:* Wrap both bodies in self._mutation_guard() and append claim-released when cancelling a task with an active lease.
- **R2-0158** `engine/runtime/bro_orchestration_runtime_v1.py:124` — V1 wrappers validate the lease and then record the transition as unproven-caller-claim *Fix:* Forward lease_id=lease_id in the four V1 delegations and drop the redundant _require_lease call, with a V1 test asserting ACTOR_ASSIGNEE_LEASE.
- **R2-0159** `engine/runtime/bro_policy.py:271` — receipt_fresh crashes on a tracked file deleted from the worktree, the case read_all was written to handle *Fix:* Catch OSError in tree_identity (or receipt_fresh) and return (False, 'tracked file unreadable: ...') so the reread path reports it by name.
- **R2-0160** `engine/runtime/bro_protected.py:211` — The writability probe creates a file inside the digest scope it is guarding *Fix:* Exclude the probe name in is_digest_member, or probe with a name already excluded (under __pycache__ semantics) and fail loudly if it cannot be remov…
- **R2-0161** `engine/runtime/bro_receipt.py:110` — exit_code accepts a boolean, and False passes as a passing run *Fix:* Reject bool for exit_code and refuse finished_at_epoch > now + 60.
- **R2-0162** `engine/runtime/bro_recovery.py:144` — previous_record_hash is a required field of the signed recovery record and nothing ever compares it *Fix:* Compare it to the canonical hash of the stored previous state in prepare_mutation, or remove the field from the shape and schema.
- **R2-0163** `engine/runtime/bro_recovery.py:237` — prove_recovery takes a root parameter and snapshots the module ROOT instead *Fix:* Use snapshot(root).
- **R2-0164** `engine/runtime/bro_release_v3.py:41` — A missing or malformed release grant escapes as ContractError; the except arm for JSONDecodeError can never fire *Fix:* Catch ContractError in _signed (or load through a local reader as _signed_env does) and add a test with a missing grant file.
- **R2-0165** `engine/runtime/bro_release_v3.py:199` — Settlement re-validates grant expiry and HEAD, so a grant that expires during the push leaves its nonce reserved *Fix:* At settlement verify signature and bindings but not expiry, or quarantine the nonce when settlement validation fails.
- **R2-0166** `engine/runtime/bro_security.py:328` — Read-only git revisions and flag values are surfaced as path targets, so the read scope gate denies `git show HEAD` and `git log -n 5` *Fix:* Have analyze_git surface only tokens after `--` plus -C/--git-dir/--work-tree values for read-only subcommands, and consume the value of value-taking…
- **R2-0167** `engine/runtime/bro_stop_controller.py:83` — On a POSIX system without /proc a live group is reported stopped *Fix:* When /proc is missing, treat a successful killpg(pgid, 0) as alive.
- **R2-0168** `engine/runtime/brops_evidence_store.py:72` — On Windows any os.link error, and on POSIX EACCES/EPERM, is treated as 'this volume cannot hardlink' *Fix:* On nt test the specific unsupported error codes, and drop EACCES from the POSIX set so a real permission failure propagates.
- **R2-0169** `engine/runtime/brops_evidence_store.py:219` — A crash in the O_EXCL fallback leaves a partial target that makes that artifact unpublishable forever *Fix:* In the fallback write to a second O_EXCL temp and os.replace only when the target is absent, or let _verify_idempotent replace a target that is a str…
- **R2-0170** `engine/runtime/brops_protocol.py:27` — is_base64url accepts a trailing newline: the regex is ^...$ under .match, the defect brops_canonical was fixed for on 2026-10-01 *Fix:* Use fullmatch (or \A...\Z) and add the trailing-newline case to the test.
- **R2-0171** `engine/runtime/brops_protocol.py:70` — strict_loads lets RecursionError escape: a deeply nested frame under the 256 KiB cap is not a ProtocolError *Fix:* Catch RecursionError in strict_loads and raise ProtocolError, with a test that a nested frame under the cap is a refusal.
- **R2-0172** `engine/runtime/brops_protocol.py:81` — encode_frame emits NaN/Infinity, which strict_loads on the other side refuses *Fix:* Pass allow_nan=False and convert the ValueError to ProtocolError.
- **R2-0173** `engine/runtime/challenge_authority.py:251` — requested_at_ms has no upper bound, so the authority signs a timestamp its own design range forbids *Fix:* Apply the 1..2^53-1 range to requested_at_ms (and to the clock values) in the authority.
- **R2-0174** `engine/runtime/challenge_authority_server.py:229` — The issue op ignores unknown fields while create-pending refuses them *Fix:* Refuse an issue request whose key set is not exactly {op, pending_challenge_id}.
- **R2-0175** `engine/runtime/challenge_authority_server.py:327` — accept_one() runs outside the loop's try, so a failure while accepting ends the service and leaks the accepted socket *Fix:* Put the accept inside the try, close the raw socket when the wrapper cannot be built, and continue the loop.
- **R2-0176** `engine/runtime/floor_writer.py:663` — commit_state ignores os.write's return value, so a short write is fsynced and renamed over the good state *Fix:* Loop until all bytes are written (or compare the returned count with len(payload)) and refuse before the rename on any shortfall.
- **R2-0177** `engine/runtime/floor_writer.py:1118` — _exchange leaks the socket when connect fails and can report 'unreachable (None)' *Fix:* Close the socket in the connect failure path and format the exception with str(exc).
- **R2-0178** `engine/runtime/governed_staging_upload.py:580` — No staging handler reads a clock: an expired challenge keeps uploading and publishing until a sweep deletes its row *Fix:* Pass clock_ms to the three staging handlers and refuse when now_ms is past the parent row's challenge_expires_at_ms.
- **R2-0179** `engine/runtime/governed_staging_upload.py:890` — Any store failure is reported to the peer as a divergent publish, and nothing reaches the operator *Fix:* Catch only the store's divergent-refuse error for that reason and let any other exception become a SupervisorError so it is printed on stderr.
- **R2-0180** `engine/runtime/governed_staging_upload.py:895` — Final tolerates an upper-case store handle, then the ledger rejects it as a supervisor fault *Fix:* Compare the handle exactly (no lower()) and refuse handle_not_challenge, or pass the recomputed digest to finalize_session.
- **R2-0181** `engine/runtime/governed_staging_upload.py:1155` — A raising on_pass callback ends the sweep schedule the docstring says cannot end *Fix:* Wrap the on_pass call in its own try/except so a failing reporter cannot retire the sweep.
- **R2-0182** `engine/runtime/governed_supervisor_ledger.py:513` — Public advance() takes any legal edge, so the launch gate CAN be bypassed, against gate_and_start's own docstring *Fix:* Restrict advance() to the refusal/terminal targets its docstring names (BLOCKED, FAILED, EXPIRED, RECOVERY_REQUIRED) and refuse the rest by name.
- **R2-0183** `engine/runtime/governed_supervisor_server.py:636` — A replayed accept-open rebuilds the 'original' lease from current config, not from the persisted lease bytes *Fix:* Decode the persisted lease_payload_bytes for the replay reply instead of mixing row fields with config fields.
- **R2-0184** `engine/runtime/governed_supervisor_server.py:792` — Terminal record and receipt are published to the protected store before the ledger decides whether the completion is legal *Fix:* Check the attempt's state and lease window before publishing, or publish inside the path that has already recorded the completion.
- **R2-0185** `engine/runtime/governed_supervisor_server.py:1177` — accept_one runs outside the loop's try, so an accept or peer-credential failure ends serve_forever and leaks the socket *Fix:* Move accept_one() inside the try and close the accepted socket in accept_socket_conn when SocketPeerConn construction fails.
- **R2-0186** `engine/runtime/isolated_signer.py:884` — Chain-agreement compares with Python ==, so a record carrying a float or a boolean 'agrees' with an attested integer *Fix:* Require type(document[field]) is type(expected) (with bool excluded) before comparing values.
- **R2-0187** `engine/runtime/isolated_signer_server.py:363` — accept_one() sits outside the try, so one failing accept ends the signer's loop its docstring says nothing can end *Fix:* Wrap accept_one() in the loop's try (retry on OSError, log, continue) and close the raw socket in accept_socket_conn when SocketPeerConn construction…
- **R2-0188** `engine/runtime/provision_floor_writer.py:151` — A very large numeric --caller-group raises an uncaught OverflowError *Fix:* Add OverflowError to the except tuple in resolve_group.
- **R2-0189** `engine/runtime/provision_floor_writer.py:468` — --reprovision crashes with a TypeError on a damaged-but-parseable store, the exact case it exists to discard *Fix:* Treat a roster that is not a list of strings as `["<unreadable>"]`, the same answer an unparseable file already gets.
- **R2-0190** `engine/runtime/provision_floor_writer.py:585` — main() promises exit codes 2/3/4 but any OSError or ValueError leaves as a traceback with exit 1, after the store has already been rewritten *Fix:* Catch OSError/ValueError in main and map them to EXIT_CUSTODY with the path named, and write the config before the state or say in the receipt which…
- **R2-0191** `engine/schemas/runtime-dependency.schema.json:23` — Schema says a dependency version MUST NOT be empty but accepts an empty string; kind is enforced only by the schema *Fix:* Add minLength:1 (and a non-whitespace pattern) to version so the schema enforces its own sentence.
- **R2-0192** `engine/schemas/task-contract.schema.json:29` — Task-contract schema accepts duplicate done_criteria and duplicate verification/rollback commands that the runtime validator refuses *Fix:* Add uniqueItems:true to the three arrays (and to contracts/task-contract.schema.json, the source copy), and extend ContractSchemaAgreementTests beyon…
- **R2-0193** `engine/tests/test_deploy_preflight.py:119` — BRO_EXECUTION_RECEIPTS is a store the runtime reads and the preflight never checks; the deriving test's regex cannot see it *Fix:* Add BRO_EXECUTION_RECEIPTS to LEDGER_VARS and derive the test's set from every name passed to an external-path helper rather than from a name-suffix…
- **R2-0194** `engine/tests/test_evidence_chain.py:317` — A missing registry escapes validate_evidence_chain as a raw SignatureError and the test was loosened to accept it *Fix:* Wrap load_trusted_keys in _resolved_keys as CompletionError and tighten the test back to CompletionError with a message assertion.
- **R2-0195** `engine/tests/test_governed_run_attestation.py:193` — SupervisorConfig accepts any string as challenge_registry_handle / challenge_registry_hash; fixtures pass 'reg-handle' and 'reg-hash' and they are recorded into the acceptance row *Fix:* Validate both registry values with _is_lower_sha256_hex in SupervisorConfig and make the fixtures use real digests.
- **R2-0196** `engine/tests/test_hooks_subprocess.py:176` — On a standalone engine checkout the class fixture overwrites the real config/trusted-keys.json, contrary to its own docstring *Fix:* Always run the drills against the built fixture copy, or qualify the docstring and write the registry only when cls.root is not ENGINE_ROOT.
- **R2-0197** `engine/tools/bro_authorize_specialist.py:99` — The mode-grant producer signs with whatever key it is given; every sibling tool checks the authority first *Fix:* Refuse a key whose authority_type is not 'issuer' before signing.
- **R2-0198** `engine/tools/bro_backup.py:197` — The manifest hashes the source after copying it, and the archive is never verified before GREEN *Fix:* Hash the archived copy and run verify_archive on dest before printing GREEN.
- **R2-0199** `engine/tools/bro_backup.py:329` — A crafted unsigned manifest crashes verify/restore with a traceback instead of RED *Fix:* Validate the manifest's shape in _load_manifest and raise BackupError for any missing or mistyped member.
- **R2-0200** `engine/tools/bro_backup.py:438` — Backup with no --source and restore with no --target report GREEN having done nothing *Fix:* Refuse an empty source or target set with a BackupError.
- **R2-0201** `engine/tools/bro_bind_workspace.py:163` — main catches only WorkspaceError and SignatureError; a missing manifest or unwritable --out is a traceback *Fix:* Add ProtectedScopeError, OSError and KeyError to the handled set so the tool prints RED and returns 1.
- **R2-0202** `engine/tools/bro_run_receipt.py:82` — A failed `git worktree add` leaks the temporary snapshot parent directory *Fix:* Move the worktree add inside the try block.
- **R2-0203** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:388` — Builder bundle hashes skills at <worktree>/skills, which does not exist in this repository layout *Fix:* Pass the engine root inside the worktree as `root`, and test the bundle with at least one real skill.
- **R2-0204** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:493` — A failing stop-registry write leaves the builder running with its lease and no teardown *Fix:* Move the register call inside the try so the finally tears the group down.
- **R2-0205** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:618` — The per-run session-state directory is never removed *Fix:* Remove state_dir in the same finally block that removes lease_dir.
- **R2-0206** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:618` — run_task never removes its bro-sup-state- directory on any path *Fix:* Remove state_dir in the same finally block that removes the lease and bundle directories.
- **R2-0207** `engine/tools/bro_supervisor.py:660` — The error path deletes the incident ledger and leaks on any exception other than SupervisorError/OSError *Fix:* Keep stop_dir when it holds incidents.jsonl, and turn contract-shape errors into SupervisorError.
- **R2-0208** `engine/tools/broctl.py:218` — A keydir can hold one key per authority, so the CLI cannot build a registry with two agent-bound builder keys; a stray JSON crashes it *Fix:* Name key files by key_id, and validate each loaded key's shape with a SignatureError.
- **R2-0209** `engine/tools/brops_receipt_signer.py:206` — The timestamp check uses int(), which accepts spaces, signs, underscores and non-ASCII digits, and signs the string verbatim *Fix:* Require `re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]+", value)` before converting.
- **R2-0210** `engine/tools/brops_supervisor_service.py:103` — Run state is read and fully re-validated twice per request, and the returned output comes from the first read while the receipt covers the second *Fix:* Let produce_sign_request accept the already-validated RunState, or return it alongside the request.
- **R2-0211** `engine/tools/brops_supervisor_service.py:121` — An unreachable signer and an unprovisioned signer are both reported as `malformed` *Fix:* Add distinct reasons (e.g. signer_unavailable, signer_unprovisioned) to the result contract.
- **R2-0212** `tools/check_action_pins.py:161` — Rule 2 asks for a trailing version comment and accepts any text; the vendored globs read *.yml only *Fix:* Require the comment to match `v?\d+(\.\d+)*` and add the *.yaml globs for the vendored trees.
- **R2-0213** `tools/check_artwork_geometry.py:107` — Rule 6 says every colour literal is on the token table; only six-digit hex is read *Fix:* Refuse any fill/stroke/stop-color value that is not none, a url(#id) or a six-digit hex.
- **R2-0214** `tools/check_artwork_geometry.py:272` — Rule 3 only walks *-light.svg, and rule 11 is skipped when README.md is absent *Fix:* Report any sheet that is not one half of a light/dark pair, and make a missing README.md a problem.
- **R2-0215** `tools/check_audit_actor.py:64` — The audit-actor gate does not see audit::record_with_payload, the second audited-write entry point *Fix:* Match `audit::record(_with_payload)?\(` and count both in the call-site total.
- **R2-0216** `tools/check_audit_actor.py:66` — The hand-built-Actor rule depends on field order, and a constructor given a literal person is not looked at *Fix:* Match either field order and check string literals passed to Actor constructors against SYSTEM_LITERALS.
- **R2-0217** `tools/check_c1_tokens.py:572` — The reduced-motion exemption regex swallows 13.6 KB of ordinary rules out of the clobber check *Fix:* Find the block end by brace matching instead of the first newline-then-brace.
- **R2-0218** `tools/check_canon_budget.py:149` — A ceiling that is not a JSON int silently switches the per-file budget off *Fix:* Refuse any per_file_bytes value that is not a positive int (and not bool) before comparing.
- **R2-0219** `tools/check_capabilities.py:322` — An L2 command may claim native-confirm or soft-delete and be allowed with nothing checking the claim *Fix:* Run the NATIVE_CONFIRM_ENFORCEMENT check for every tier that claims native-confirm and require a named enforcement for soft-delete.
- **R2-0220** `tools/check_contrast.py:265` — The WCAG floor is read from the manifest being graded, and nothing pins it *Fix:* Hold normal>=4.5 and large>=3.0 as constants in the gate and refuse a manifest that states less or adds another size.
- **R2-0221** `tools/check_contrast.py:368` — The palette-mirror precondition is skipped when the same manifest is named by an absolute path *Fix:* Compare resolved paths, or always run the mirror check unless a --no-mirror test flag is given.
- **R2-0222** `tools/check_contrast.py:368` — The palette-mirror precondition runs only when --manifest is spelled exactly as the default, and reads tokens relative to the cwd *Fix:* Resolve the manifest and compare resolved paths against ROOT/DEFAULT_MANIFEST (or add an explicit --no-mirror for fixtures), and pass ROOT instead of…
- **R2-0223** `tools/check_control_invocation.py:170` — A quoted filename in another control counts as an invocation, so a compile list credits eight tools with blocking a merge *Fix:* Count only an import or a subprocess/argv use as invocation, and record a compile-only relation as such.
- **R2-0224** `tools/check_control_invocation.py:235` — `blocks: deploy` and `blocks: release` are accepted with nothing derived to back them *Fix:* Derive deploy/release from the installer and release workflow the way merge is derived, or refuse the two values until something can.
- **R2-0225** `tools/check_coordination.py:348` — A 'Last updated' line the gate could not read in the parent commit passes, and the GREEN line says it moved *Fix:* Treat 'unreadable' as could-not-compare (a problem) and print a separate sentence for 'absent'.
- **R2-0226** `tools/check_coordination.py:832` — Four live TASKS rows are outside the status and one-ID-one-row checks, and the status check passes on any word in the row *Fix:* Match `T-\d+[a-z]?`, collect every bold id in the first cell, and test the status cell rather than the whole line.
- **R2-0227** `tools/check_coordination.py:881` — When git cannot produce the PR diff the state-sync guard is skipped in silence; the sibling gate fails closed on the same condition *Fix:* When GITHUB_BASE_REF is set and the diff cannot be computed, return RED, and run git with -C root.
- **R2-0228** `tools/check_doc_claims.py:149` — The ticket rule's alphabet and digit count leave the current audit round's findings and O-1..O-5 unchecked *Fix:* Derive the id shapes from the ticket sources (letters seen there, 1-4 digits, optional letter suffix) instead of a fixed class.
- **R2-0229** `tools/check_doc_claims.py:545` — The cited_in check passes on any occurrence of the digits, so small counted claims cannot go red *Fix:* Declare for each claim the phrase or anchor the number must appear in and match that, not the bare number.
- **R2-0230** `tools/check_negative_matrix.py:118` — Only the last symbol of a test reference is looked up; the class or module in the middle is never verified *Fix:* Resolve each `::` segment in order (class, then method) and refuse a missing one.
- **R2-0231** `tools/check_no_assumptions.py:53` — The `assum` hedge misses 'assumes' and 'assumptions' — the dead-stem defect this file already fixed for `reproduc`/`mutat` *Fix:* Use `\bassum\w*` and add both sentences to the gate's test.
- **R2-0232** `tools/check_no_owner_key_ceremony.py:48` — ROOT is taken from sys.argv[1] at import time, in two gates *Fix:* Resolve the root inside main() with argparse `--root`, as the other gates do.
- **R2-0233** `tools/check_no_owner_key_ceremony.py:185` — prose() removes `*` and backticks but not `_` emphasis, and tokens are matched case-sensitively *Fix:* Strip `_` used as emphasis in prose() and compare tokens case-insensitively.
- **R2-0234** `tools/check_principal_model.py:46` — The gate holds three documents to 'seven'; five state the count *Fix:* Derive the document set by grepping docs/ for the claim instead of a fixed tuple, or add the two missing files.
- **R2-0235** `tools/check_prior_art.py:61` — stem_collisions walks .claude/worktrees/ copies and then demands every hit be named *Fix:* Enumerate with `git ls-files` (or skip `.claude/worktrees/`), and require one named collision for generic stems rather than all.
- **R2-0236** `tools/check_produced_artifact.py:303` — The 'produced, not committed' control switches itself off when git cannot answer *Fix:* Return None only when `root` is not inside a work tree; refuse on any other git failure.
- **R2-0237** `tools/check_produced_artifact.py:613` — Two gates default --root to the cwd instead of the repository *Fix:* Default both to the file's repository root.
- **R2-0238** `tools/check_reachability.py:262` — is_python_test and is_rust_test test the ABSOLUTE path — the defect `skipped()` was rewritten to remove *Fix:* Take `root` and test `path.relative_to(root).parts`, as `skipped()` does.
- **R2-0239** `tools/check_reachability.py:682` — A gate counts as 'executed by a workflow' if its path appears anywhere in the file, comments included *Fix:* Apply the same same-line rule: the path must sit on a line containing `python`.
- **R2-0240** `tools/check_reachability.py:683` — A gate named only in a YAML comment counts as 'executed by a workflow' (G-08 was fixed for tests only) *Fix:* Apply the same co-occurrence rule to gates: count a gate only on a non-comment line that also contains `python`.
- **R2-0241** `tools/check_read_receipt.py:122` — A shell with no session id shares one receipt named 'unknown', contrary to what CLAUDE.md says a bare run means *Fix:* Refuse (exit 1, 'no session id') instead of falling back to a shared name.
- **R2-0242** `tools/check_release_signing.py:339` — Nothing requires release.yml to run --verify-updater-signatures, the only step that reads the produced bytes *Fix:* Add a rule requiring a non-comment `--verify-updater-signatures` run line in the build job.
- **R2-0243** `tools/check_repo_state.py:668` — The REST road collapses a missing `draft` to False — the fail-open compare_external_prs forbids *Fix:* Return `pr.get("draft")` unconverted so a missing value reaches the existing refusal.
- **R2-0244** `tools/check_repo_state.py:729` — Any stderr containing '403' is treated as a permission gap, so a rate-limit 403 skips the branch-protection comparison *Fix:* Skip only on 'Resource not accessible by integration'; treat 'rate limit' text as an outage and refuse.
- **R2-0245** `tools/check_repo_state.py:1004` — --root moves the snapshot but not git or gh: every live question is asked of the process's cwd *Fix:* Pass `cwd=root` (or `git -C root`) to every git and gh subprocess.
- **R2-0246** `tools/check_residual_items.py:95` — Rule 6 compares only the summary table's owner-secret cell; its Severity and Status cells are checked against nothing *Fix:* Compare cells 1 and 2 with the section's Severity and Status the same way.
- **R2-0247** `tools/check_roadmap_order.py:162` — A duplicate status-board row is silently dropped; the 'count check' the comment cites does not exist *Fix:* Collect every row per phase number and report a phase that has more than one.
- **R2-0248** `tools/check_roadmap_order.py:253` — The out-of-order exemption is read from the working tree; nothing checks it is committed *Fix:* Read the exemption from `git show HEAD:config/roadmap-order-exemptions.json` (or origin/main) and reject a non-integer expiry.
- **R2-0249** `tools/check_runbook_snippets.py:27` — 'The runbooks' is one document; the engine's operator runbook pastes owned-module calls that nothing checks *Fix:* Add the operator runbook to DOCS and derive CHECKED_MODULES from engine/runtime/*.py.
- **R2-0250** `tools/check_runbook_snippets.py:134` — The cited-path check sees 2 of the runbook's repo paths; any path written with a checkout prefix is skipped *Fix:* Strip the known checkout prefixes (`~/OS/`, `$HOME/OS/`) before matching and check directories too, so the count reflects what the document cites.
- **R2-0251** `tools/check_source_control_bytes.py:42` — Tracked text outside SWEPT_EXT is never swept, including a root-run install script and the commit hook *Fix:* Sweep every tracked file that is not on a short binary-extension list instead of an allowlist of text extensions.
- **R2-0252** `tools/check_source_control_bytes.py:76` — render() does not make DEL visible although the sweep reports it *Fix:* Treat 0x7F like the other control bytes in render().
- **R2-0253** `tools/check_source_control_bytes.py:119` — A DECLARED exception excuses every occurrence of that byte in the file, not the one fixture *Fix:* Declare the expected occurrence count per entry and fail when the measured count differs.
- **R2-0254** `tools/check_spec_references.py:42` — Section citations in Cargo manifests and hooks are outside the scan *Fix:* Add `.toml` to SCAN_SUFFIXES and `.claude` to SCAN_DIRS.
- **R2-0255** `tools/check_spec_references.py:155` — A named test 'exists' if its name is a substring of any source file; five declared names are no longer real test names *Fix:* Match `fn|def <name>\b` (or an it/test title) instead of a substring, and update the five renamed entries.
- **R2-0256** `tools/check_state_fields.py:132` — A field also counts as read when it appears as `[field]` in any reader file *Fix:* Drop the bracket form and require the quoted name.
- **R2-0257** `tools/check_token_parity.py:38` — Default paths are relative to the working directory, unlike the other gates, and a missing motion key is a traceback *Fix:* Resolve defaults from `pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]` and use _require for the motion keys.
- **R2-0258** `tools/check_truncated_lines.py:98` — The gate prints GREEN when it scanned no file *Fix:* Return RED when the globs match zero files.
- **R2-0259** `tools/generate_agent_definitions.py:196` — tools_for() falls open: a record granting nothing gets Edit and Write *Fix:* Return TOOLS_BUILD only when `can_build` is true and TOOLS_NO_EDIT otherwise.
- **R2-0260** `tools/generate_negative_matrix.py:146` — A case row whose ID cell is not an exact ID is skipped silently, against the parser's stated rule *Fix:* Raise ParseError when a table row's ID cell contains `NM-` but does not match ID_RE.
- **R2-0261** `tools/stamp_pr_head.py:153` — Running the tool from any branch other than the PR's reports 'Push first' when nothing is unpushed *Fix:* Compare `git rev-parse <branch>` (or say which branch is checked out) before claiming unpushed work.
- **R2-0262** `tools/sync_active_pr.py:156` — The mirror is restored only on SystemExit; a ValueError after the first write leaves it half-rewritten *Fix:* Restore on any exception (`except BaseException`) or convert those lookups into SystemExit refusals.
- **R2-0263** `tools/sync_active_pr.py:192` — Three subprocess calls have no timeout in the tool whose own comment records that a hung gh hung the settle *Fix:* Pass timeout=60 and turn TimeoutExpired/CalledProcessError into the existing RED refusals.
- **R2-0264** `tools/sync_active_pr.py:660` — rewrite_carrier_block's `open`/`merged` rewrites are dead on the real mirror, and would overwrite an unrelated empty string if one appeared *Fix:* Rewrite only keys present in the block (as `current` already is), drop the `open`/`merged` entries, and refuse when the old text is absent, as swap()…
- **R2-0265** `tools/sync_active_pr.py:889` — --banner bypasses the size bound in --settled mode but not in the normal mode *Fix:* Wrap the whole expression in _bounded() in settle() as main() does.

## 4. Found by the Builder afterwards

Not from a reader. Open unless the line says otherwise.

- **B-01** `tools/check_doc_claims.py:545` — a counted claim is "cited" in a document when the number occurs anywhere in it as a standalone number. `tasks_archive_2026_09_rows` was 72 and then 73 and passed both times although the archive never stated its row count: the digits were there by accident (the `T-154` row says "73 mutants, 73 named deaths"). At 74 no accident matched and the gate went RED, which is how it was found. **The archive now states its count (fixed in the pull request that added this line); the gate's matching is unchanged and still open** — *Fix:* require the number next to the words the claim is about, per claim.
- **B-02** `tools/check_merge_ready.py:233` — the merge gate told an auditor to file a per-change report under `engine/AUDIT/changes/`. Every Markdown file under `engine/` must be registered in `engine/config/documentation-manifest.json` (`engine/tools/bro_docs_freshness.py`), which is an audit-required path, so registering the report is a change the same gate refuses after the audited head: the engine suite went red on the Architect's first report for `#328`. **Fixed in `#328`:** the gate now names `apps/desktop/AUDIT/changes/`, and the report was moved there unchanged.
- **B-03** `engine/tests/test_live_sudoers_install.py` — the Architect's host, which has a real `visudo`, reported two failures there ("real `visudo` rejects wildcard command arguments"). The Builder's box has no `visudo`, so that branch has never run here. The Architect's third report for `#328` carries the output: that host's `visudo` answers `syntax error: wildcards are not allowed in command arguments` for the kits' `governed_recorder` rule, which passes `--out /opt/brops-live/report/live-*.out` and three more globbed arguments. Debian 13's sudo 1.9.16 accepts it. **Open:** if that host runs a sudo that forbids wildcards in arguments — the Builder's guess, not checked, is `sudo-rs` — the live kits cannot install their sudoers fragment there at all.
- **B-04** `engine/tests` — the Architect's host has no `python` on `PATH` (only `python3`) and three tests fail there for that reason (`test_bytecode_shadow`, `test_live_hook_deny`, `test_env_health`). The Windows machine of B-05 has neither `python` nor `python3` on `PATH` and fails five tests for it. **Open:** whether the tests or the wired command line should stop assuming `python`.
- **B-05** `engine/tests/test_signature_authority.py` (`OperatorRootPinTests.test_the_rewrite_question_is_answered_for_owners_that_are_not_this_account`) — on a real Windows machine under an account named `Admin`, three subtests fail: a descriptor granting `FILE_WRITE_DATA`, `DELETE` or `WRITE_DAC` "to this token was read as un-rewritable — the refusal would not apply". CI's `windows-latest` passes the same test. If the reading is wrong on that kind of account, the operator-root-pin refusal does not fire there. **Open, and the highest-priority item in this section:** not reproduced by the Builder, who has no Windows; the transcript is `apps/desktop/AUDIT/changes/pr-328-windows-run-relayed.md`.
- **B-06** `engine/tests/test_control_plane_writable.py` (`test_the_probe_beats_os_access_where_they_disagree`) — same machine: `['runtime', 'runtime/sub'] != []`. **Open:** not reproduced; whether the probe or the test is wrong for an administrator account is not known.
- **B-07** `engine/tests/test_live_provisioning_anchor.py` (`test_every_live_shell_script_parses`) — same machine: both kits fail to parse with an EMPTY message. **Open:** the Builder's guess, marked as one, is that `bash` there is the WSL launcher with no distribution; the test should say which `bash` it ran and print what it printed.
- **B-08** `engine/runtime/bro_audit_log.py::append` — every append reads and (since `#328`) walks the whole chain, so its cost grows with the ledger and nothing bounds, rotates or checkpoints it. Measured by the Builder on Debian 13: 1,000 records 11 ms; 5,000 records 58 ms; 20,000 records 225 ms; 100,000 records (34 MB) 1.17 s per append, of which `read_all` 0.40 s and the chain walk 0.70 s. The lock timeout is 10 s. **Open:** a rotation or checkpoint design, which is the Architect's question 10 on `#328`.
- **B-09** `engine/runtime/bro_audit_log.py::_signer_argv` — the signing command's first word is resolved through symbolic links before it is run. A virtualenv's `python` IS a symbolic link to the base interpreter, so a signer started as `<venv>/bin/python signer.py` runs under the base interpreter and loses the virtualenv's packages. On the Architect's host (a `uv` virtualenv, Python 3.12) 31 tests fail for it: the stand-in signer cannot import `cryptography`. CI and the Builder's box install into the interpreter itself and never see it. It fails closed. **Open:** whether to pin the path as given and check the resolved one, or to say that a signer must not live in a virtualenv; and the tests should not depend on which of the two the host is.
